Implemented ✅ — `Go Back` now returns to the same settings area where `Test` was triggered.

### What changed

1. **Preserved test origin section in navigation flow**
   - `onTestInApp` now carries a section id (`String`) through settings layers.
   - Ref: @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/SettingsScreen.kt#323-325, @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/SettingsScreen.kt#2553-2557

2. **Passed section from color editor test actions**

   - Refs:
     - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/SettingsScreen.kt#2940-2944
     - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/SettingsScreen.kt#3080-3084

3. **Stored and replayed return target in MainActivity**
   - Added:
     - `settingsReturnSection`
     - `settingsReturnRequestId`
   - On `Test`, store section + leave settings.
   - On `Go Back`, increment request id + reopen settings.
   - Refs:
     - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#838-842
     - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#3413-3419

     - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#3615-3619

4. **Re-focused settings area on return**
   - [SettingsScreen](file:///c:/Users/javie/AndroidStudioProjects/TeXter2025/app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/SettingsScreen.kt:147:0-812:1) now reacts to `settingsReturnRequestId` and expands matching category.

### User Input

Works! Now when the user has a note opened, there are the content and memo areas. Currently the user can expand the content area downwards until the show/hide memo button disappears from the screen. And if the user shrinks the content area; the memo button appears again. The problem is now that if the user taps on the memo button to open it, the memo area does not get visible because the content area already fills the bottom screen area. Now what he has to do is to manually shrink the content area until he sees the memo area. Than if the user closes the memo area, the content memo area will keep shrunk (because of the space where the memo area was) and the user needs to manually expand again if he wants to make it bigger. 
So the solution i would like to have is to implement the function that as soon as the user opens the memo area, the content area should be shrunk at the size that the memo area fits in the screen depending on the set size the user set it. Than if the user hides the memo area, than the content area should be automatically be expanded full but only until the poiint before the show/hide memo area button (because here the button should be visible. If it should not be visible, the user just can manually expand the content area more).

### Planner Response

Implemented exactly that behavior in the opened-note editor.

### ✅ What now happens

- **When user taps “Show Memo”**:
  - Content area is **auto-shrunk** just enough so memo can appear in view.
- **When user taps “Hide Memo”**:
  - Content area is **auto-expanded** to a safe max that keeps the memo toggle row visible.
- **While memo is visible**:
  - Manual content drag can’t exceed a memo-safe max height (prevents pushing memo out of view again).

### Code changes

- Added memo/content auto-layout logic and bounds in:
  - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/FullScreenEditor.kt#201-255
- Measured available editor height for accurate dynamic calculations:
  - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/FullScreenEditor.kt#351-355
- Constrained content drag max height when memo is shown:
  - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/FullScreenEditor.kt#464-467
- Applied auto-shrink/expand directly in memo toggle handler:
  - @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/FullScreenEditor.kt#607-627

### Planner Response

Implemented.

### What changed

I added a spring-based height animation for the content area so memo show/hide transitions feel soft/elastic, while preserving responsive manual drag:

- Added drag-state gate: `isContentAreaDragging`  
  @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/FullScreenEditor.kt#228-228
- Wired drag lifecycle to toggle that state:
  - `onDragStart` → true
  - `onDragEnd` / `onDragCancel` → false  
  @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/FullScreenEditor.kt#442-465
- Animated height fraction with spring and applied it only when not actively dragging:  
  @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/FullScreenEditor.kt#498-520

This keeps drag behavior immediate (no lag during handle drag), but memo-toggle auto-resize now animates.

---

### Verification

Build passed successfully:

- Ran: `./gradlew assembleDebug`
- Result: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`

(Only existing warnings, no new errors.)

### Planner Response

Root cause fixed.

### What was broken
Delete actions were firing in UI, but they were effectively **no-op** because [SettingsScreen](file:///c:/Users/javie/AndroidStudioProjects/TeXter2025/app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/SettingsScreen.kt:147:0-812:1) delete callbacks were not wired from [MainActivity](file:///c:/Users/javie/AndroidStudioProjects/TeXter2025/app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt:52:0-4055:1) (so defaults ran).  
That matches your logs: long-click path runs, but no state mutation happens.

### Fix applied
I added the missing delete callback bindings in [MainActivity](file:///c:/Users/javie/AndroidStudioProjects/TeXter2025/app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt:52:0-4055:1)’s [SettingsScreen(...)](file:///c:/Users/javie/AndroidStudioProjects/TeXter2025/app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/ui/components/SettingsScreen.kt:147:0-812:1) call for all sections:

- `onDeleteContentAreaColor` @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#1923-1946  
- `onDeleteMemoAreaColor` @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#2041-2064  
- `onDeleteBgColor` @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#2255-2278  
- `onDeleteFileListItemColor` @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#2423-2446  
- `onDeleteFileListBackgroundColor` @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#2586-2609  

Each one removes the exact square by `(color + normalized photoUri)` and persists preset updates.

### Validation
- `./gradlew assembleDebug` passed.

### Docs updated
- Changelog entry added: @CHANGELOG.md#13-39  
- Chat history entry added: @CHAT_HISTORY.md#3-25  

I followed the global memories/rules on minimal root-cause fixes and mandatory doc updates each session.

### User Input

I noticed a bug, when tapping on any background color square and than on the "test" button, i get redirected to the main view of the app with the entries and floating button of "go back" what is correct, than if i tap on the hamburger icon to open the settings, than i can read all clearly what is also correct and if i tap on the floating icon of "go back", i get redirected to the preview area i was before.
But if i open a text file/entry and than from there the floating "go back" button, than i get redirected to the previous area i was but i see all blured. Than if i go back using the back function of my smartphone, than i get into the normal main view of the app (withou the "go back") button and when i open the settings hamburger, than all is clear.

### Planner Response

Implemented a root-cause fix for the blur persistence path.

### What I changed
The blur on main scaffold depends on:

- `isFullScreenEditorShown && customBgBlur > 0f` @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#1617-1622

In the floating **Go Back** callback used by [FileManagementApp](file:///c:/Users/javie/AndroidStudioProjects/TeXter2025/app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt:4058:0-4386:1), I now explicitly clear the full-screen editor state **before** switching to settings:

- `isFullScreenEditorShown = false` added @app/src/main/java/com/j4/texter2025/MainActivity.kt#3604-3608

### Why this fixes it
Your reported path (`Settings -> Test -> Main -> open note -> Go Back`) could leave `isFullScreenEditorShown` stale true during transition, so scaffold blur kept applying. Resetting it in that callback guarantees the blur gate is off when returning.
